=== add test/overnight/dry-run.mjs
new cbf6e2cd2b1654b21995b623
+/**
+ * Dry run of the overnight compaction test. Drives the agent through 2-3
+ * commits of Spring PetClinic via the /api/chat endpoint, captures snapshots
+ * and diffs, and writes a report.
+ *
+ * Same shape as the full overnight runner — just a smaller N.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/overnight/dry-run.mjs [maxCommits]
+ *
+ * Prerequisites:
+ *   - Dev server running on port 28889 with PROJECT_DIR pointing at petclinic
+ *   - PetClinic checked out at the dry-run-start tag (or its starting commit)
+ *   - JAVA_HOME pointing at Java 21
+ */
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { readFile, writeFile, mkdir, cp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
+const PROJECT = path.join(ROOT, 'test-fixtures', 'petclinic');
+const OUT = path.join(__dirname, 'dry-run-output');
+const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 28889);
+const MAX = Number(process.argv[2] ?? 3);
+const STARTING_COMMIT = '0c88f91';
+const TURN_TIMEOUT_MS = 45 * 60 * 1000;     // 45 min cap per commit — real petclinic commits routinely take 7+ min per agent turn × 5-10 turns
+const CANCEL_WAIT_MS  = 90 * 1000;          // after /api/cancel, wait up to 90s for status to transition away from 'running'
+
+// ── Subprocess helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+async function run(cmd, args, cwd = PROJECT) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    const p = spawn(cmd, args, { cwd, shell: true });
+    let out = '', err = '';
+    p.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d.toString('utf8'); });
+    p.stderr.on('data', (d) => { err += d.toString('utf8'); });
+    p.on('close', (code) => resolve({ code, out: out.trim(), err: err.trim() }));
+  });
+}
+
+async function gitShow(commit) {
+  const msg = (await run('git', ['show', commit, '--format=%s%n%n%b', '--no-patch'])).out;
+  const diff = (await run('git', ['show', commit, '--format=', '--stat'])).out;
+  const full = (await run('git', ['show', commit, '--format=', '-U2'])).out;
+  return { sha: commit, message: msg, stat: diff, fullDiff: full };
+}
+
+async function ensureBaseline() {
+  await run('git', ['reset', '--hard', STARTING_COMMIT]);
+  await run('git', ['clean', '-fdx']);
+}
+
+// ── Chat via new API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// POST /api/message kicks off the agent (returns immediately). We then poll
+// /api/state until status returns to done/error. Returns the events collected.
+async function sendChat(text) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  // Note: we intentionally do NOT reset between commits any more. Compaction
+  // demotion only fires on PRIOR tasks — never the current one. With a reset
+  // per commit, every commit's T-1 is current and never gets demoted, so the
+  // entire tier-2/3/4 machinery never runs. Letting the session accumulate
+  // across commits is also closer to how a real user works (one session,
+  // many asks). The script's initial /api/reset (in main()) still clears
+  // state at the very start of the run.
+  // Capture the chatEvents count BEFORE so we can slice "this commit's
+  // events" from the post-chat snapshot. Limit=10000 returns ALL events
+  // (chatStateSnapshot returns all when limit ≥ length); a big-enough cap
+  // means we get a true count rather than a tail-only slice.
+  const preState = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state?limit=10000`).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null);
+  const preEventCount = preState?.events ? preState.events.length : 0;
+
+  const res = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, {
+    method: 'POST',
+    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+    body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6' }),
+    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
+  });
+  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);
+
+  // Poll state until done/error or timeout.
+  const deadline = Date.now() + TURN_TIMEOUT_MS;
+  let state = null;
+  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
+    const s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json());
+    state = s;
+    if (s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error') break;
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
+  }
+  if (!state || state.status === 'running') {
+    // Timed out — ask server to cancel, then wait for the agent loop to
+    // unwind. The abort signal is best-effort (the dev SDK adapter doesn't
+    // propagate it into the in-flight LLM call), so the loop may need a
+    // bit of time to detect the abort and transition to 'error'. Without
+    // this wait, the next /api/message call gets 409 "chat is already
+    // running" and every subsequent commit fails.
+    await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/cancel`, { method: 'POST' });
+    const cancelDeadline = Date.now() + CANCEL_WAIT_MS;
+    while (Date.now() < cancelDeadline) {
+      const s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null);
+      if (!s || s.status !== 'running') break;
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
+    }
+    throw new Error('chat timeout');
+  }
+  if (state.status === 'error') {
+    throw new Error(`chat error: ${state.error || 'unknown'}`);
+  }
+
+  // events is the FULL accumulated chat-event list (no per-commit reset).
+  // Slice to just this commit's events using the pre-call count so the
+  // per-commit metrics (events=, assistantText=) are still meaningful.
+  const allEvents = state.events || [];
+  const events = allEvents.slice(preEventCount);
+  const assistantText = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).join('\n');
+  return { assistantText, events, latencyMs: Date.now() - t0 };
+}
+
+async function getSnapshot() {
+  const res = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/snapshot`);
+  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`snapshot HTTP ${res.status}`);
+  return await res.json();
+}
+
+// ── Diff comparison ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+function diffStats(diffText) {
+  let add = 0, del = 0, files = new Set();
+  for (const line of diffText.split('\n')) {
+    if (line.startsWith('+++') || line.startsWith('---')) continue;
+    if (line.startsWith('+')) add++;
+    else if (line.startsWith('-')) del++;
+    const m = line.match(/^diff --git a\/(\S+)/);
+    if (m) files.add(m[1]);
+  }
+  return { add, del, files: [...files] };
+}
+
+function classify(agent, real) {
+  const a = diffStats(agent), r = diffStats(real);
+  if (a.add === 0 && a.del === 0) return 'NO_CHANGES';
+  if (agent.trim() === real.trim()) return 'PERFECT_MATCH';
+  // Same files touched?
+  const sameFiles = a.files.length > 0 && a.files.every((f) => r.files.includes(f));
+  const closeStats = Math.abs(a.add - r.add) <= Math.max(3, r.add * 0.3) &&
+                     Math.abs(a.del - r.del) <= Math.max(3, r.del * 0.3);
+  if (sameFiles && closeStats) return 'EQUIVALENT_LIKELY';
+  if (sameFiles) return 'SAME_FILES_DIFFERENT_LOGIC';
+  return 'DIFFERENT';
+}
+
+// ── Main loop ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+async function main() {
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+  await mkdir(path.join(OUT, 'snapshots'), { recursive: true });
+  await mkdir(path.join(OUT, 'diffs'), { recursive: true });
+  await mkdir(path.join(OUT, 'agent-tasks'), { recursive: true });
+
+  console.log(`[dry-run] starting; max=${MAX} commits`);
+  await ensureBaseline();
+
+  // Reset session state on the server (clear events, tasks)
+  await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/reset`, { method: 'POST' });
+
+  // Get agenda
+  const { out: agenda } = await run('git', ['log', '--reverse', '--oneline', `${STARTING_COMMIT}..origin/main`]);
+  const commits = agenda.split('\n').slice(0, MAX).map((line) => {
+    const [sha, ...rest] = line.trim().split(' ');
+    return { sha, title: rest.join(' ') };
+  });
+  console.log(`[dry-run] agenda:`);
+  for (const c of commits) console.log(`  - ${c.sha} ${c.title}`);
+
+  const report = [];
+
+  for (const c of commits) {
+    console.log(`\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════`);
+    console.log(`[commit] ${c.sha} — ${c.title}`);
+    console.log(`══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════`);
+
+    // Capture the real commit details
+    const real = await gitShow(c.sha);
+
+    // Build a prompt. Give ONLY the commit message body. No diff.
+    const prompt = [
+      `Please implement this change to the project:`,
+      ``,
+      real.message,
+      ``,
+      `Make only the changes needed for this. Keep it focused. When you're done, briefly say what you did.`,
+    ].join('\n');
+
+    const turnStart = Date.now();
+    let result;
+    try {
+      result = await sendChat(prompt);
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error(`  [error] chat failed: ${e.message}`);
+      // Capture evidence BEFORE ensureBaseline wipes everything. The agent
+      // may have made real edits and built up task-store state — we want it.
+      try {
+        const partialDiff = (await run('git', ['diff', '-U2'])).out;
+        await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'diffs', `${c.sha}-agent-PARTIAL.diff`), partialDiff);
+      } catch (cap) { console.error(`  [error] diff capture failed: ${cap.message}`); }
+      try {
+        await cp(path.join(PROJECT, '.code_boss', 'tasks'),
+                 path.join(OUT, 'agent-tasks', `${c.sha}-PARTIAL`),
+                 { recursive: true });
+      } catch (cap) { console.error(`  [error] tasks capture failed: ${cap.message}`); }
+      // Snapshot from the server (may be partial)
+      try {
+        const snap = await getSnapshot();
+        await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'snapshots', `${c.sha}-PARTIAL.json`), JSON.stringify(snap, null, 2));
+      } catch {}
+      const elapsed = Date.now() - turnStart;
+      console.error(`  [error] captured partial state after ${elapsed}ms`);
+      report.push({ sha: c.sha, title: c.title, status: 'CHAT_FAILED', error: e.message, elapsedMs: elapsed });
+      await ensureBaseline();
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    const turnMs = Date.now() - turnStart;
+    console.log(`  [chat] ${turnMs}ms · events=${result.events.length} · text=${result.assistantText.length}ch`);
+
+    // Capture state
+    const snapshot = await getSnapshot();
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'snapshots', `${c.sha}.json`), JSON.stringify(snapshot, null, 2));
+
+    // Copy agent's tasks dir
+    try {
+      await cp(path.join(PROJECT, '.code_boss', 'tasks'), path.join(OUT, 'agent-tasks', c.sha), { recursive: true });
+    } catch {}
+
+    // Get the diff the agent produced
+    const agentDiff = (await run('git', ['diff', '-U2'])).out;
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'diffs', `${c.sha}-agent.diff`), agentDiff);
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'diffs', `${c.sha}-real.diff`), real.fullDiff);
+
+    // mvn compile check (best-effort)
+    let compileResult = 'SKIPPED';
+    try {
+      const javaHome = 'C:\\Program Files\\Eclipse Adoptium\\jdk-21.0.11.10-hotspot';
+      const mvn = await new Promise((resolve) => {
+        const p = spawn('mvn', ['-q', '-DskipTests', '-o', 'compile'], {
+          cwd: PROJECT,
+          shell: true,
+          env: { ...process.env, JAVA_HOME: javaHome, PATH: `${javaHome}\\bin;${process.env.PATH}` },
+        });
+        let err = '';
+        p.stderr.on('data', (d) => { err += d.toString('utf8'); });
+        let timer = setTimeout(() => { try { p.kill(); } catch {} }, 5 * 60 * 1000);
+        p.on('close', (code) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ code, err: err.slice(0, 2000) }); });
+      });
+      compileResult = mvn.code === 0 ? 'COMPILE_OK' : `COMPILE_FAIL(${mvn.code})`;
+    } catch (e) {
+      compileResult = `COMPILE_ERROR: ${e.message}`;
+    }
+
+    const cls = classify(agentDiff, real.fullDiff);
+    const aStats = diffStats(agentDiff);
+    const rStats = diffStats(real.fullDiff);
+
+    console.log(`  [compile] ${compileResult}`);
+    console.log(`  [diff   ] agent: +${aStats.add}/-${aStats.del} across ${aStats.files.length} files`);
+    console.log(`  [diff   ] real:  +${rStats.add}/-${rStats.del} across ${rStats.files.length} files`);
+    console.log(`  [class  ] ${cls}`);
+
+    // Pull compaction info from snapshot
+    const events = snapshot?.events ?? [];
+    const compactEvents = events.filter((e) => e.summary?.includes('compact') || e.type === 'compact');
+    const llmClusterEvents = events.filter((e) => e.summary?.includes('LLM cluster') || e.summary?.includes('cluster'));
+    console.log(`  [compact] ${compactEvents.length} compact event(s), ${llmClusterEvents.length} LLM cluster call(s)`);
+    console.log(`  [tokens ] total=${snapshot.totalTokens ?? '?'}`);
+
+    report.push({
+      sha: c.sha,
+      title: c.title,
+      latencyMs: turnMs,
+      events: result.events.length,
+      assistantText: result.assistantText.slice(0, 500),
+      agentDiff: { add: aStats.add, del: aStats.del, files: aStats.files },
+      realDiff:  { add: rStats.add, del: rStats.del, files: rStats.files },
+      compileResult,
+      classification: cls,
+      totalTokens: snapshot.totalTokens,
+      compactEvents: compactEvents.length,
+      llmClusterCalls: llmClusterEvents.length,
+    });
+
+    // Reset the FILES to ground truth for next iteration, but preserve
+    // .code_boss/ so the agent's task store (and accumulating conversation
+    // history) survives across commits. Without --exclude here, git clean
+    // -fdx would wipe the dir on every commit, defeating the no-reset
+    // change we just made above.
+    console.log(`  [reset  ] checkout ${c.sha}`);
+    await run('git', ['reset', '--hard', c.sha]);
+    await run('git', ['clean', '-fdx', '--exclude=.code_boss']);
+  }
+
+  // Final report
+  const reportPath = path.join(OUT, 'report.md');
+  const lines = ['# Dry Run Report', '', `Time: ${new Date().toISOString()}`, `Project: petclinic`, `Commits: ${commits.length}`, ''];
+  for (const r of report) {
+    lines.push(`## ${r.sha} — ${r.title}`);
+    lines.push('');
+    if (r.status === 'CHAT_FAILED') {
+      // Chat never reached the classification stage; emit the failure
+      // details we DO have instead of a wall of "undefined".
+      lines.push(`- **status:** CHAT_FAILED`);
+      lines.push(`- error: ${r.error ?? 'unknown'}`);
+      lines.push(`- elapsed before failure: ${r.elapsedMs ?? '?'}ms`);
+      lines.push('');
+      continue;
+    }
+    lines.push(`- classification: **${r.classification}**`);
+    lines.push(`- compile: ${r.compileResult}`);
+    lines.push(`- latency: ${r.latencyMs}ms`);
+    lines.push(`- events: ${r.events}`);
+    lines.push(`- agent diff: +${r.agentDiff?.add ?? '?'}/-${r.agentDiff?.del ?? '?'} (${r.agentDiff?.files?.length ?? 0} files)`);
+    lines.push(`- real diff:  +${r.realDiff?.add ?? '?'}/-${r.realDiff?.del ?? '?'} (${r.realDiff?.files?.length ?? 0} files)`);
+    lines.push(`- compact events: ${r.compactEvents}, LLM cluster calls: ${r.llmClusterCalls}`);
+    lines.push(`- total tokens: ${r.totalTokens}`);
+    lines.push('');
+    lines.push('**Agent said:**');
+    lines.push('');
+    lines.push('> ' + (r.assistantText || '(no text)').split('\n').join('\n> '));
+    lines.push('');
+  }
+  await writeFile(reportPath, lines.join('\n'));
+  console.log(`\n[dry-run] wrote report: ${reportPath}`);
+}
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('[dry-run] FATAL', e); process.exit(1); });
+
=== add test/pii-contest/_ai.mjs
new 2f8ea16b2292c2f01aae7a02
+/**
+ * THE AI ROUND — a live model reads, answers questions about, and edits files masked by each contestant.
+ *
+ *   node test/pii-contest/_ai.mjs [--arms original,piiscan,piishape#,piishape§,mask-everything] [--reps 1] [--temp 0]
+ *   node test/pii-contest/_ai.mjs --rescore <run dir> [--home <piiscan home>]   re-score saved replies, no new calls
+ *
+ * Arms:
+ *   original         the file as it is — the ceiling
+ *   piiscan          detect-and-mask (the shipped plugin)
+ *   piishape# / §    mask everything, reveal what is safe — with the # marker the user proposed, and with §
+ *   mask-everything  every run masked — the floor
+ *
+ * Scoring:
+ *   questions  a judge (same model; it sees neither the file nor the arm) scores each answer against the reference:
+ *              2 correct, 1 partial, 0 wrong or "cannot tell"
+ *   edits      the model's FIND/REPLACE blocks are mapped back through the arm's own unmasking and applied to the
+ *              ORIGINAL file; a mechanical check decides pass/fail — right place, right change, nothing else
+ *              touched, no mask token written into the file
+ *   tokens     the prompt tokens the endpoint reports for each question call
+ *
+ * Output: .cache/ai/<stamp>/ — every masked file, reply and verdict, results.json and summary.json.
+ */
+import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..', '..');
+const toUrl = (p) => 'file:///' + p.replace(/\\/g, '/');
+const arg = (k, d) => { const i = process.argv.indexOf('--' + k); return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : d; };
+
+const RESCORE = arg('rescore', null);
+const DIR = RESCORE ? path.resolve(RESCORE) : path.join(HERE, '.cache', 'ai', new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-'));
+mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+// piiscan's tokens are an HMAC under a per-install secret kept in its home directory, so a run's replies can only be
+// mapped back with the same home. It lives inside the run directory now; --home points a rescore at an older one.
+const HOME = arg('home', path.join(DIR, 'home'));
+mkdirSync(HOME, { recursive: true });
+
+const PS = await import(toUrl(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'piiscan__1.mjs')));
+const { createAnthropicAdapter } = await import(toUrl(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'llm', 'anthropic-http.mjs')));
+const { diffLines } = await import(toUrl(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'agent', 'diff.mjs')));
+const { LIVE_MODEL } = await import(toUrl(path.join(ROOT, 'test', '_live-model.mjs')));
+const { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } = await import(toUrl(path.join(ROOT, 'test', '_proxy-creds.mjs')));
+const { FILES } = await import('./ai-tasks.mjs');
+const { createShapeMasker } = await import('./piishape.mjs');
+
+const REPS = Number(arg('reps', 1));
+const TEMP = Number(arg('temp', 0));
+const DRY = process.argv.includes('--dry');
+const CONCURRENCY = Number(arg('concurrency', 4));
+// Run-2 prompt change, applied to every masked arm alike: the first run found the model inventing a token for a value
+// it wanted to write ('F', beside a masked 'M'). A token can only ever be copied; a new value is written as itself.
+const NEVER_INVENT = arg('invent-note', '1') === '1';
+
+const ARTIFACT = /\[PII:[A-Z]+#[0-9a-f]{8}\]|[#§][Xx0]+[#§][0-9a-z]{7}/;
+
+function makeArm(name) {
+  const tail = NEVER_INVENT ? ' Never make up a token: to write a new value, write the value itself.' : '';
+  if (name === 'original') return { name, note: 'The file is shown exactly as it is.', mask: (t) => t, unmask: (t) => t };
+  if (name === 'piiscan') {
+    return {
+      name,
+      note: 'Personal data in this file may have been replaced by redaction tokens such as [PII:NAME#a1b2c3d4] — the kind of value, and a hash of it; the same value always gets the same token. When you quote or edit text, copy these tokens exactly and they will be restored to the real value.' + tail,
+      mask: (t, f) => PS.maskText(t, { filePath: f, homeDir: HOME }),
+      unmask: (t) => PS.unmaskText(t, {}),
+    };
+  }
+  const m = name === 'piishape§' ? createShapeMasker({ marker: '§' })
+    : name === 'mask-everything' ? createShapeMasker({ marker: '#', revealNothing: true })
+      : createShapeMasker({ marker: '#' });
+  return { name, note: m.describe() + tail, mask: (t, f) => m.maskText(t, { filePath: f }), unmask: (t) => m.unmaskText(t) };
+}
+
+const qaPrompt = (arm, file, masked) => `You are an experienced software engineer reviewing someone else's code.
+
+File: ${file.path}
+${arm.note}
+
+<<<FILE
+${masked}
+FILE>>>
+
+Answer each question from this file alone. Be specific and brief. If the file does not let you determine an answer, say "cannot tell".
+Reply with JSON only, in exactly this shape: {"answers": ["answer 1", "answer 2", "answer 3"]}
+
+${file.qa.map(([q], i) => `${i + 1}. ${q}`).join('\n')}`;
+
+const editPrompt = (arm, file, masked, edit) => `You are an experienced software engineer making a change to someone else's code.
+
+File: ${file.path}
+${arm.note}
+
+<<<FILE
+${masked}
+FILE>>>
+
+Make this change: ${edit.instruction}
+
+Reply ONLY with one or more edit blocks in exactly this form, and nothing else:
+<<<<<<< FIND
+(text copied exactly from the file above — enough whole lines to be unique)
+=======
+(the replacement text)
+>>>>>>> REPLACE`;
+
+const judgePrompt = (file, answers) => `You are grading answers to questions about a source file. For each question, compare the candidate answer with the reference answer.
+Score 2 if the candidate is correct and complete, 1 if it is partly correct or missing a detail, 0 if it is wrong, missing, or says it cannot tell.
+Reply with JSON only, in exactly this shape: {"scores": [n, n, n]}
+
+${file.qa.map(([q, ref], i) => `Question ${i + 1}: ${q}\nReference: ${ref}\nCandidate: ${answers[i] ?? '(no answer)'}`).join('\n\n')}`;
+
+/**
+ * The LAST balanced JSON object in a reply that has the wanted key. A model that answers, writes "wait, let me
+ * recheck", and answers again is giving its final answer second — and a greedy /\{.*\}/ spanning both objects parses
+ * as nothing at all, which scored three correct answers as zero in the first run.
+ */
+function lastJsonWith(s, key) {
+  const t = String(s || '');
+  const found = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++) {
+    if (t[i] !== '{') continue;
+    let depth = 0, inStr = false, esc = false;
+    for (let j = i; j < t.length; j++) {
+      const c = t[j];
+      if (inStr) { if (esc) esc = false; else if (c === '\\') esc = true; else if (c === '"') inStr = false; continue; }
+      if (c === '"') inStr = true;
+      else if (c === '{') depth++;
+      else if (c === '}') { depth--; if (depth === 0) { try { const o = JSON.parse(t.slice(i, j + 1)); if (o && key in o) found.push(o); } catch { /* not JSON */ } break; } }
+    }
+  }
+  return found.length ? found[found.length - 1] : null;
+}
+
+function applyEdits(arm, before, reply) {
+  const blocks = [...String(reply).matchAll(/<<<<<<< FIND\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n=======\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n?>>>>>>> REPLACE/g)];
+  if (!blocks.length) return { ok: false, why: 'no edit blocks' };
+  let text = before;
+  for (const b of blocks) {
+    let find, repl;
+    try { find = arm.unmask(b[1]); repl = arm.unmask(b[2]); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, why: 'unmask refused: ' + String(e.message).slice(0, 80) }; }
+    const n = text.split(find).length - 1;
+    if (n !== 1) return { ok: false, why: `FIND matched ${n} times in the real file` };
+    text = text.replace(find, () => repl);
+  }
+  if (ARTIFACT.test(text) && !ARTIFACT.test(before)) return { ok: false, why: 'a mask token was written into the file', after: text };
+  return { ok: true, after: text };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Line counts for a check, ignoring what does not change behaviour. A blank line left where two lines were deleted,
+ * or a "# Server" comment above a new property, is a correct edit — the first run failed both, for every arm
+ * including the unmasked original, which is how the check was found to be wrong rather than the model.
+ */
+function lineDelta(before, after) {
+  const d = { adds: 0, dels: 0, codeAdds: 0, codeDels: 0 };
+  for (const x of diffLines(before, after)) {
+    if (x.type === 'same' || !x.line.trim()) continue;
+    const comment = /^\s*(#|\/\/|--|\/\*|\*)/.test(x.line);
+    if (x.type === 'add') { d.adds++; if (!comment) d.codeAdds++; } else { d.dels++; if (!comment) d.codeDels++; }
+  }
+  return d;
+}
+
+function judgeEdit(arm, original, edit, reply) {
+  const applied = applyEdits(arm, original, reply);
+  if (!applied.ok) return { ok: false, why: applied.why };
+  const d = lineDelta(original, applied.after);
+  const ok = !!edit.check(original, applied.after, d);
+  return { ok, why: ok ? '' : `applied, but the result is wrong (+${d.adds}/-${d.dels})` };
+}
+
+async function pool(items, fn) {
+  const out = new Array(items.length);
+  let next = 0;
+  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(CONCURRENCY, items.length) }, async () => {
+    while (next < items.length) { const i = next++; out[i] = await fn(items[i], i); }
+  }));
+  return out;
+}
+
+function summarize(results, arms, reps) {
+  const qa = results.filter((r) => r.kind === 'qa');
+  const summary = {};
+  for (const armName of arms) {
+    const q = qa.filter((r) => r.arm === armName);
+    const e = results.filter((r) => r.kind === 'edit' && r.arm === armName);
+    const points = q.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.scores || [0, 0, 0]).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), 0);
+    const max = q.reduce((s, r) => s + 2 * FILES.find((f) => f.id === r.file).qa.length, 0);
+    summary[armName] = {
+      qaScore: max ? points / max : 0,
+      byFile: Object.fromEntries(FILES.map((f) => { const rs = q.filter((r) => r.file === f.id); const p = rs.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.scores || [0, 0, 0]).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), 0); return [f.id, rs.length ? p / (2 * f.qa.length * rs.length) : null]; })),
+      edits: { pass: e.filter((r) => r.ok).length, total: e.length, failures: e.filter((r) => !r.ok).map((r) => `${r.key || r.arm} ${r.edit}: ${r.why}`) },
+      promptTokens: q.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.promptTokens || 0), 0) / Math.max(1, reps),
+    };
+  }
+  const base = summary.original?.promptTokens || 0;
+  const col = (s, w) => String(s).padEnd(w);
+  console.log('\nRESULTS\n');
+  console.log(col('', 20) + arms.map((a) => col(a, 18)).join(''));
+  console.log(col('questions', 20) + arms.map((a) => col((summary[a].qaScore * 100).toFixed(0) + '%', 18)).join(''));
+  console.log(col('edits', 20) + arms.map((a) => col(`${summary[a].edits.pass}/${summary[a].edits.total}`, 18)).join(''));
+  console.log(col('prompt tokens', 20) + arms.map((a) => col(`${Math.round(summary[a].promptTokens)}${base ? ` (${((summary[a].promptTokens / base - 1) * 100).toFixed(0)}%)` : ''}`, 18)).join(''));
+  console.log('\n  questions by file');
+  for (const f of FILES) console.log('  ' + col(f.id, 18) + arms.map((a) => col(summary[a].byFile[f.id] == null ? '—' : (summary[a].byFile[f.id] * 100).toFixed(0) + '%', 18)).join(''));
+  for (const a of arms) if (summary[a].edits.failures.length) console.log(`\n  ${a} edit failures:\n    ` + summary[a].edits.failures.join('\n    '));
+  return summary;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+  const call = async (content, max_tokens, temperature = TEMP) => {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const r = await adapter.complete({ model: LIVE_MODEL, messages: [{ role: 'user', content }], max_tokens, temperature });
+    return { text: r?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '', promptTokens: r?.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+  };
+  const judgeAll = (qa) => pool(qa, async (r) => {
+    const file = FILES.find((f) => f.id === r.file);
+    try {
+      const j = await call(judgePrompt(file, r.answers || []), 400, 0);
+      const s = lastJsonWith(j.text, 'scores');
+      r.scores = Array.isArray(s?.scores) ? s.scores.map((n) => Math.max(0, Math.min(2, Number(n) || 0))) : [0, 0, 0];
+      r.judgeReply = j.text;
+    } catch (e) { r.scores = [0, 0, 0]; r.judgeError = String(e.message); }
+  });
+
+  if (RESCORE) {
+    const results = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(DIR, 'results.json'), 'utf8'));
+    const keys = [...new Set(results.map((r) => r.key).filter(Boolean))];
+    const arms = [...new Set(results.map((r) => r.arm))];
+    const prepared = {};
+    for (const key of keys) {
+      const arm = makeArm(key.split('#').slice(0, -1).join('#') || key);
+      prepared[key] = { arm, originals: {} };
+      for (const f of FILES) {
+        const o = f.text();
+        prepared[key].originals[f.id] = o;
+        const now = arm.mask(o, f.path);
+        // A rescore is only faithful if the maskers still produce what the model was shown.
+        const saved = path.join(DIR, `${key.replace('#', '.')}.${f.id}.masked.txt`);
+        if (existsSync(saved) && readFileSync(saved, 'utf8') !== now) console.log(`  WARNING ${key} ${f.id}: masked text differs from the run's — its edits may not map back`);
+      }
+    }
+    for (const r of results) {
+      const p = prepared[r.key];
+      if (!p) continue;
+      if (r.kind === 'edit') {
+        const f = FILES.find((x) => x.id === r.file), e = f.edits.find((x) => x.id === r.edit);
+        Object.assign(r, judgeEdit(p.arm, p.originals[r.file], e, r.reply));
+      } else if (r.kind === 'qa') {
+        const parsed = lastJsonWith(r.reply, 'answers');
+        r.answers = Array.isArray(parsed?.answers) ? parsed.answers.map(String) : [];
+      }
+    }
+    await judgeAll(results.filter((r) => r.kind === 'qa'));
+    const reps = new Set(keys.map((k) => k.split('#').pop())).size || 1;
+    const summary = summarize(results, arms, reps);
+    writeFileSync(path.join(DIR, 'results-rescored.json'), JSON.stringify(results, null, 1));
+    writeFileSync(path.join(DIR, 'summary-rescored.json'), JSON.stringify({ rescored: true, summary }, null, 1));
+    console.log('\nre-scored in', DIR);
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const ARMS = arg('arms', 'original,piiscan,piishape#,piishape§,mask-everything').split(',');
+  const jobs = [];
+  for (const armName of ARMS) {
+    for (let rep = 1; rep <= REPS; rep++) {
+      const arm = makeArm(armName);
+      const key = `${armName}#${rep}`;
+      for (const f of FILES) {
+        const original = f.text();
+        const masked = arm.mask(original, f.path);
+        writeFileSync(path.join(DIR, `${armName}.${rep}.${f.id}.masked.txt`), masked);
+        jobs.push({ kind: 'qa', key, arm, file: f, masked });
+        for (const e of f.edits) jobs.push({ kind: 'edit', key, arm, file: f, masked, original, edit: e });
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  console.log(`model ${LIVE_MODEL}, temp ${TEMP}, arms ${ARMS.join(' ')}, reps ${REPS}: ${jobs.length} model calls + judging`);
+  if (DRY) { console.log('dry run — masked files written to', DIR); return; }
+
+  const results = await pool(jobs, async (j) => {
+    try {
+      if (j.kind === 'qa') {
+        const r = await call(qaPrompt(j.arm, j.file, j.masked), 900);
+        const parsed = lastJsonWith(r.text, 'answers');
+        const answers = Array.isArray(parsed?.answers) ? parsed.answers.map(String) : [];
+        process.stdout.write(`  qa   ${j.key.padEnd(20)} ${j.file.id.padEnd(18)} ${r.promptTokens} tok\n`);
+        return { kind: 'qa', key: j.key, arm: j.arm.name, file: j.file.id, reply: r.text, answers, promptTokens: r.promptTokens };
+      }
+      const r = await call(editPrompt(j.arm, j.file, j.masked, j.edit), 1800);
+      const { ok, why } = judgeEdit(j.arm, j.original, j.edit, r.text);
+      process.stdout.write(`  edit ${j.key.padEnd(20)} ${j.edit.id.padEnd(4)} ${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL ' + why}\n`);
+      return { kind: 'edit', key: j.key, arm: j.arm.name, file: j.file.id, edit: j.edit.id, reply: r.text, ok, why, promptTokens: r.promptTokens };
+    } catch (e) {
+      process.stdout.write(`  ERR  ${j.key} ${j.file.id} ${String(e.message).slice(0, 100)}\n`);
+      return { kind: j.kind, key: j.key, arm: j.arm.name, file: j.file.id, edit: j.edit?.id, error: String(e.message), ok: false, why: 'call failed', answers: [] };
+    }
+  });
+  await judgeAll(results.filter((r) => r.kind === 'qa'));
+  const summary = summarize(results, ARMS, REPS);
+  writeFileSync(path.join(DIR, 'results.json'), JSON.stringify(results, null, 1));
+  writeFileSync(path.join(DIR, 'summary.json'), JSON.stringify({ model: LIVE_MODEL, temp: TEMP, reps: REPS, neverInventNote: NEVER_INVENT, home: HOME, summary }, null, 1));
+  console.log('\nwritten to', DIR);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/pii-contest/_contest.mjs
new 746a923ae85318ffa1f95a4c
+/**
+ * THE CONTEST — piiscan (detect PII, mask it) against piishape (mask everything, reveal what is safe).
+ *
+ *   node test/pii-contest/_contest.mjs recall [seeds...]   how much planted PII each one hides
+ *   node test/pii-contest/_contest.mjs static               what each one costs a reader of ordinary code
+ *
+ * RECALL scores every planted value two ways:
+ *   hidden       not one of its letters or digits is visible
+ *   no-id-leak   nothing identifying is visible — a run of 3+ letters or 4+ digits of the value, or any part of an
+ *                e-mail's local part. A visible "Way" in "5840 Cedar Way" or "gov" in an address is not identifying;
+ *                a visible surname or the last four of an SSN is.
+ *
+ * STATIC measures, on real repositories with no planted data: the share of letters and digits hidden (everything
+ * hidden there is collateral, as far as anyone can tell), and how much longer the masked text is (a proxy for tokens,
+ * confirmed with real token counts in the AI round).
+ *
+ * Results go to stdout and to .cache/results/<name>.json.
+ */
+import { mkdirSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..', '..');
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-contest-home-'));
+const PS = await import(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'piiscan__1.mjs').replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^([A-Za-z]):/, 'file:///$1:'));
+const { createShapeMasker, familyOf, STREET_SUFFIXES } = await (async () => {
+  const shape = await import('./piishape.mjs');
+  const vocab = await import('./vocab.mjs');
+  return { ...shape, STREET_SUFFIXES: vocab.STREET_SUFFIXES };
+})();
+
+// ── the contestants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+function contestants() {
+  const piiscan = {
+    name: 'piiscan',
+    spans: (text, file) => {
+      const cls = PS.classifyFile(file);
+      if (cls.cls === 'skip') return [];
+      return PS.findMatches(text, { cls: cls.cls, locale: PS.isLocaleBundle(file), allow: () => false }).map((m) => ({ start: m.start, end: m.end }));
+    },
+    mask: (text, file) => PS.maskText(text, { filePath: file, homeDir: HOME }),
+  };
+  const shapeHash = createShapeMasker({ marker: '#', name: 'piishape#' });
+  const shapeSect = createShapeMasker({ marker: '§', name: 'piishape§' });
+  const floor = createShapeMasker({ marker: '#', revealNothing: true, name: 'mask-everything' });
+  const wrap = (m) => ({ name: m.name, spans: (t, f) => m.maskSpans(t, { filePath: f }), mask: (t, f) => m.maskText(t, { filePath: f }), masker: m });
+  return [piiscan, wrap(shapeHash), wrap(shapeSect), wrap(floor)];
+}
+
+const ALNUM = /[\p{L}\p{N}\p{M}]/u;
+const pct = (a, b) => (b ? (100 * a / b).toFixed(1) + '%' : '—');
+
+function maskedBitmap(len, spans) {
+  const b = new Uint8Array(len);
+  for (const s of spans) b.fill(1, Math.max(0, s.start), Math.min(len, s.end));
+  return b;
+}
+
+/** What of a planted value is still readable? */
+function leakOf(text, bitmap, v) {
+  let visible = 0, total = 0;
+  const visibleRuns = [];
+  let cur = '', curStart = -1;
+  for (let i = v.start; i < v.end; i++) {
+    const ch = text[i];
+    const isA = ALNUM.test(ch);
+    if (isA) total++;
+    if (isA && !bitmap[i]) { visible++; if (curStart < 0) curStart = i; cur += ch; }
+    else if (cur) { visibleRuns.push([curStart, cur]); cur = ''; curStart = -1; }
+  }
+  if (cur) visibleRuns.push([curStart, cur]);
+  let identifying = false;
+  if (v.type === 'EMAIL') {
+    const at = v.start + v.value.indexOf('@');
+    identifying = visibleRuns.some(([s]) => s < at);
+  } else if (v.type === 'DOB') {
+    // A month name on its own identifies nobody; four digits of a date — a year, or a day and a month — do.
+    identifying = visibleRuns.reduce((n, [, r]) => n + (r.match(/\p{N}/gu) || []).length, 0) >= 4;
+  } else {
+    identifying = visibleRuns.some(([, r]) => {
+      if (v.type === 'ADDR' && (STREET_SUFFIXES.has(r.toLowerCase()) || /^[NSEW]$/i.test(r))) return false;
+      return /\p{L}{3,}/u.test(r) || /\p{N}{4,}/u.test(r);
+    });
+  }
+  return { visible, total, identifying, visibleText: visibleRuns.map(([, r]) => r).join(' ') };
+}
+
+function recall(seeds) {
+  const all = contestants();
+  const results = {};
+  for (const c of all) results[c.name] = { values: 0, hidden: 0, noIdLeak: 0, byTier: {}, byType: {}, byCtx: {}, leaks: [], ms: 0 };
+  for (const seed of seeds) {
+    const dir = path.join(HERE, '.cache', 'planted', String(seed));
+    const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(dir, 'manifest.json'), 'utf8'));
+    const byFile = new Map();
+    // AN IP ADDRESS IS NOT SCORED. The developer's call (2026-10-09): addresses are not personal data here and are
+    // shown on purpose - masking them hid the one thing a failed connection needs. They stay planted (so the files
+    // look the way such files look), but a shown address is not counted as a leak, and a hidden one not as a catch.
+    // NOR IS A DATE (the developer, 2026-10-22: "Let's unmask dates everywhere using all common date formats") - a
+    // date of birth included: dates are shown so the agent can order them.
+    for (const v of manifest) { if (v.type === 'IP' || v.type === 'DOB') continue; if (!byFile.has(v.file)) byFile.set(v.file, []); byFile.get(v.file).push(v); }
+    for (const [file, vals] of byFile) {
+      const text = readFileSync(path.join(dir, file), 'utf8');
+      for (const c of all) {
+        const t0 = Date.now();
+        const spans = c.spans(text, file);
+        const R = results[c.name];
+        R.ms += Date.now() - t0;
+        const bm = maskedBitmap(text.length, spans);
+        for (const v of vals) {
+          const L = leakOf(text, bm, v);
+          const hidden = L.visible === 0, safe = !L.identifying;
+          R.values++; if (hidden) R.hidden++; if (safe) R.noIdLeak++;
+          for (const [bucket, k] of [['byTier', v.tier], ['byType', v.type], ['byCtx', v.ctx]]) {
+            const b = R[bucket][k] || (R[bucket][k] = { n: 0, hidden: 0, noIdLeak: 0 });
+            b.n++; if (hidden) b.hidden++; if (safe) b.noIdLeak++;
+          }
+          if (!safe && R.leaks.length < 400) R.leaks.push({ seed, file, type: v.type, ctx: v.ctx, value: v.value, visible: L.visibleText });
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  // report
+  const names = all.map((c) => c.name);
+  const col = (s, w = 16) => String(s).padEnd(w);
+  console.log(`\nRECALL over seeds ${seeds.join(', ')} — ${results[names[0]].values} planted values\n`);
+  console.log(col('', 22) + names.map((n) => col(n, 26)).join(''));
+  console.log(col('no identifying leak', 22) + names.map((n) => col(pct(results[n].noIdLeak, results[n].values), 26)).join(''));
+  console.log(col('fully hidden', 22) + names.map((n) => col(pct(results[n].hidden, results[n].values), 26)).join(''));
+  for (const bucket of ['byTier', 'byType', 'byCtx']) {
+    console.log(`\n  ${bucket.slice(2)}  (no identifying leak / fully hidden)`);
+    const keys = Object.keys(results[names[0]][bucket]).sort();
+    for (const k of keys) {
+      const row = names.map((n) => { const b = results[n][bucket][k]; return col(`${pct(b.noIdLeak, b.n)} / ${pct(b.hidden, b.n)}`, 26); }).join('');
+      console.log('  ' + col(`${k} (${results[names[0]][bucket][k].n})`, 20) + row);
+    }
+  }
+  // macro average across contexts, so the CSV rows do not decide the result
+  console.log('\n  macro-average across contexts (no identifying leak)');
+  for (const n of names) {
+    const ks = Object.values(results[n].byCtx);
+    console.log(`  ${col(n, 20)} ${(ks.reduce((s, b) => s + b.noIdLeak / b.n, 0) / ks.length * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
+  }
+  for (const n of names) {
+    const leaks = results[n].leaks;
+    if (!leaks.length) continue;
+    console.log(`\n  ${n}: sample identifying leaks (${leaks.length}${leaks.length >= 400 ? '+' : ''})`);
+    for (const l of leaks.slice(0, 14)) console.log(`    ${l.type.padEnd(8)} ${l.ctx.padEnd(13)} ${JSON.stringify(l.value).padEnd(34)} visible: ${JSON.stringify(l.visible)}`);
+  }
+  save('recall', results);
+  return results;
+}
+
+function* walk(dir, depth = 0) {
+  if (depth > 12) return;
+  let ents; try { ents = readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
+  for (const e of ents) {
+    if (/^(node_modules|\.git|dist|build|target|out|vendor|\.idea|\.mvn)$/.test(e.name)) continue;
+    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
+    if (e.isDirectory()) yield* walk(p, depth + 1);
+    else yield p;
+  }
+}
+
+function staticCost() {
+  const corpora = ['D:/tmp/petclinic', 'D:/tmp/algos', 'D:/tmp/countries', 'D:/tmp/datasets', 'D:/tmp/fhir'];
+  const all = contestants();
+  const out = {};
+  const col = (s, w = 16) => String(s).padEnd(w);
+  // WORDS and DIGITS separately. A GeoJSON outline is millions of coordinate digits nobody reads; counted together,
+  // they decide the percentage and say nothing about whether the code can be understood.
+  // And only files piiscan would actually scan: it skips .svg, .map and lockfiles outright (they go to the model as
+  // they are), so counting those against one contestant and not the other would not be a comparison.
+  const RUN = /[\p{L}\p{N}\p{M}]+/gu;
+  const cell = (r) => `words ${pct(r.wHid, r.w)}, digits ${pct(r.dHid, r.d)}, +${((r.outLen / r.inLen - 1) * 100).toFixed(0)}% len`;
+  console.log('\nSTATIC COST on real repositories (nothing planted)\n');
+  console.log(col('corpus', 12) + all.map((c) => col(c.name, 44)).join(''));
+  const fam = {};
+  for (const root of corpora) {
+    const rows = Object.fromEntries(all.map((c) => [c.name, { w: 0, wHid: 0, d: 0, dHid: 0, inLen: 0, outLen: 0 }]));
+    for (const f of walk(root)) {
+      let st; try { st = statSync(f); } catch { continue; }
+      if (st.size > 1500000 || /\.(png|jpe?g|gif|ico|pdf|zip|gz|jar|class|rda|dcm|woff2?|ttf|eot|xlsx?|docx?)$/i.test(f)) continue;
+      const rel = path.relative(root, f);
+      if (PS.classifyFile(rel).cls === 'skip') continue;
+      let text; try { text = readFileSync(f, 'utf8'); } catch { continue; }
+      if (text.includes('\u0000')) continue;
+      const family = familyOf(f);
+      const runs = [...text.matchAll(RUN)].map((m) => ({ s: m.index, e: m.index + m[0].length, digits: /^\p{N}+$/u.test(m[0]) }));
+      for (const c of all) {
+        const bm = maskedBitmap(text.length, c.spans(text, rel));
+        const r = rows[c.name];
+        const fk = `${family}|${c.name}`;
+        const fr = fam[fk] || (fam[fk] = { w: 0, wHid: 0, d: 0, dHid: 0, inLen: 0, outLen: 0 });
+        for (const run of runs) {
+          let hid = 0; for (let i = run.s; i < run.e; i++) if (bm[i]) hid++;
+          const n = run.e - run.s;
+          for (const x of [r, fr]) { if (run.digits) { x.d += n; x.dHid += hid; } else { x.w += n; x.wHid += hid; } }
+        }
+        const masked = c.mask(text, rel);
+        for (const x of [r, fr]) { x.inLen += text.length; x.outLen += masked.length; }
+      }
+    }
+    out[root] = rows;
+    console.log(col(path.basename(root), 12) + all.map((c) => col(cell(rows[c.name]), 44)).join(''));
+  }
+  console.log('\n  by file family');
+  const families = [...new Set(Object.keys(fam).map((k) => k.split('|')[0]))].sort();
+  for (const f of families) console.log('  ' + col(f, 10) + all.map((c) => { const r = fam[`${f}|${c.name}`]; return col(r ? cell(r) : '—', 44); }).join(''));
+  save('static', { corpora: out, families: fam });
+}
+
+function save(name, obj) {
+  const dir = path.join(HERE, '.cache', 'results');
+  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(path.join(dir, name + '.json'), JSON.stringify(obj, null, 1));
+}
+
+const [cmd, ...rest] = process.argv.slice(2);
+if (cmd === 'recall') recall(rest.length ? rest.map(Number) : [1, 2, 3]);
+else if (cmd === 'static') staticCost();
+else { console.log('usage: _contest.mjs recall [seeds] | static'); process.exit(2); }

+    public EnrollmentResult enroll(Dependent d) {
+        if (d.ssn() == null || !SSN.matcher(d.ssn()).matches()) {
+            log.warn("rejected enrollment: invalid SSN format");
+            return EnrollmentResult.rejected("INVALID_SSN");
+        }
+        int age = Period.between(d.birthDate(), LocalDate.now()).getYears();
+        if (age < MIN_DEPENDENT_AGE || age > MAX_DEPENDENT_AGE) {
+            return EnrollmentResult.rejected("AGE_OUT_OF_RANGE");
+        }
+        String email = d.email().toLowerCase();
+        if (!enrolledEmails.add(email)) {
+            return EnrollmentResult.rejected("DUPLICATE");
+        }
+        if (enrollments.size() >= MAX_DEPENDENTS) {
+            enrolledEmails.remove(email);
+            return EnrollmentResult.rejected("SPONSOR_LIMIT");
+        }
+        log.info("enrolled dependent {}", d.email());
+        Enrollment e = new Enrollment(d, LocalDate.now());
+        enrollments.add(e);
+        return EnrollmentResult.accepted(e);
+    }
+}

=== add test/pii-contest/build-lexicon.mjs
new ca182b78bfc3b08255feb16d
+/**
+ * Builds lexicon.json for the inverted masker from public sources, cached under .cache/ (gitignored):
+ *
+ *   SCOWL via wordlist-english@1.2.1 (MIT; SCOWL's own licence is permissive) — English words, proper names excluded
+ *   Census 2010 surnames (public domain)              https://www2.census.gov/topics/genealogy/2010surnames/names.zip
+ *   Census 1990 given names (public domain)           https://www2.census.gov/topics/genealogy/1990surnames/dist.*.first
+ *   SSA baby names 1880-2008, via hadley/data-baby-names (public domain data)
+ *   Census 2023 Gazetteer places (public domain)      2023_Gaz_place_national.zip
+ *
+ * What it produces:
+ *   english      every word SCOWL lists up to size 50 (common words; proper names are not in SCOWL)
+ *   ambiguous    the English words that are also a reasonably common surname or any given name — "grant", "hope",
+ *                "rose", "mason". Revealed only where context says it is a word (see piishape.mjs)
+ *   guardNames   names and places that are NOT English words but could be assembled from them — "washington" is
+ *                "washing" + "ton". A token on this list is never split into pieces to be revealed.
+ *   jargonCodeOnly  JARGON entries that are also names (jackson, jenkins): code position only
+ *   typoNames    names of any rarity that are one letter-swap from an English word ("teh", "thier", "brian"): never
+ *                shown as a typo of that word (see typoOf in piishape.mjs)
+ *   typoNear     strings one swap from a word AND one swap from a name ("krala"): never shown as a typo either
+ *   splitNames   names of any rarity one moved letter from a word ("ewing" -> "wing"): never shown as half of a
+ *                misplaced space (see splitTypo in piishape.mjs)
+ *   delNames     names of any rarity one letter more than a word ("jones" -> "ones"): never shown as a word with an
+ *                extra letter (see extraLetterOf in piishape.mjs)
+ *   delNear      a common name with a letter typed into it that reads as a word with one too ("moorel"): the same
+ *   insNames     names of any rarity one letter less than a word ("mary" -> "marry"): never shown as a word with a
+ *                letter left out (see missingLetterOf in piishape.mjs)
+ *   insNear      a common name with a letter left out that reads as a word with one left out: the same
+ *
+ * Run: node test/pii-contest/build-lexicon.mjs
+ */
+import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { JARGON, FUNCTION_WORDS } from './vocab.mjs';
+
+const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const CACHE = path.join(HERE, '.cache');
+mkdirSync(CACHE, { recursive: true });
+
+// A surname has to be at least this common before an English word is held back for it. Tuned from the data below:
+// the 2010 list runs to 162,253 surnames at 100+ people each, and at that floor "into", "about" and "would" are
+// surnames — held back everywhere for a handful of people. See the printed sample when changing it.
+const SURNAME_MIN = Number(process.env.CB_SURNAME_MIN || 2000);
+
+async function fetchTo(url, file) {
+  if (existsSync(file)) return;
+  const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'user-agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 code_boss lexicon build' } });
+  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`fetch ${url}: ${r.status}`);
+  writeFileSync(file, Buffer.from(await r.arrayBuffer()));
+}
+
+async function sources() {
+  const wl = path.join(CACHE, 'wordlist-english');
+  if (!existsSync(path.join(wl, 'english-words-50.json'))) {
+    mkdirSync(wl, { recursive: true });
+    execFileSync('npm', ['pack', 'wordlist-english@1.2.1', '--silent'], { cwd: CACHE, shell: true });
+    // (relative to the cache: given D:\..., Windows tar took the drive letter for a remote host - 2026-10-28)
+    execFileSync('tar', ['-xzf', 'wordlist-english-1.2.1.tgz', '-C', 'wordlist-english', '--strip-components=1'], { cwd: CACHE, shell: true });
+  }
+  await fetchTo('https://www2.census.gov/topics/genealogy/2010surnames/names.zip', path.join(CACHE, 'names2010.zip'));
+  if (!existsSync(path.join(CACHE, 'names2010', 'Names_2010Census.csv'))) execFileSync('unzip', ['-o', '-q', 'names2010.zip', '-d', 'names2010'], { cwd: CACHE, shell: true });
+  await fetchTo('https://www2.census.gov/topics/genealogy/1990surnames/dist.female.first', path.join(CACHE, 'female.first'));
+  await fetchTo('https://www2.census.gov/topics/genealogy/1990surnames/dist.male.first', path.join(CACHE, 'male.first'));
+  await fetchTo('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hadley/data-baby-names/master/baby-names.csv', path.join(CACHE, 'baby-names.csv'));
+  // The full SSA national file — every name given to five or more babies a year, 1880-2017 — via TidyTuesday, since
+  // ssa.gov refuses scripted downloads. The top-1,000 file above stops at 2008, before "Legend", "Journey" and
+  // "Royal" were given to thousands of children a year, and that is exactly how they reached the recall run's leaks.
+  await fetchTo('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rfordatascience/tidytuesday/main/data/2022/2022-03-22/babynames.csv', path.join(CACHE, 'ssa-babynames-1880-2017.csv'));
+  await fetchTo('https://www2.census.gov/geo/docs/maps-data/data/gazetteer/2023_Gazetteer/2023_Gaz_place_national.zip', path.join(CACHE, 'places.zip'));
+  if (!existsSync(path.join(CACHE, 'places', '2023_Gaz_place_national.txt'))) execFileSync('unzip', ['-o', '-q', 'places.zip', '-d', 'places'], { cwd: CACHE, shell: true });
+}
+
+export function load() {
+  const wl = path.join(CACHE, 'wordlist-english');
+  const english = new Set();
+  for (const f of readdirSync(wl)) {
+    const m = /^(english|american)-words-(\d+)\.json$/.exec(f);
+    if (!m || Number(m[2]) > 60) continue;
+    for (const w of JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(wl, f), 'utf8'))) {
+      if (/'/.test(w) || /[A-Z]/.test(w)) continue;          // possessives, and OK/kW/dB
+      english.add(w.normalize('NFC'));
+    }
+  }
+  const surnames = new Map();
+  for (const line of readFileSync(path.join(CACHE, 'names2010', 'Names_2010Census.csv'), 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).slice(1)) {
+    const [name, , count] = line.split(',');
+    if (name && /^[A-Z]+$/.test(name)) surnames.set(name.toLowerCase(), Number(count) || 0);
+  }
+  // GIVEN NAMES NEED A FLOOR TOO. Both lists carry data-entry artefacts — "Unknown", "Many", "Numbers", "Manual" —
+  // and names given to a handful of children in one year ("Else", "Hence", "Author"). Held back everywhere, those
+  // mask ordinary prose for no one. So: a baby name counts if it reached 1 in 10,000 births in some year, and a
+  // 1990 Census name if it is in the top 1,000 of its list.
+  const given = new Set();
+  const everyGiven = new Set();   // with no floor: what a typo must never be (typoNames below)
+  for (const f of ['female.first', 'male.first']) {
+    for (const line of readFileSync(path.join(CACHE, f), 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)) {
+      const cols = line.trim().split(/\s+/);
+      if (cols[0] && /^[A-Z]+$/.test(cols[0])) everyGiven.add(cols[0].toLowerCase());
+      if (cols[0] && /^[A-Z]+$/.test(cols[0]) && Number(cols[3]) <= 1000) given.add(cols[0].toLowerCase());
+    }
+  }
+  const maxPct = new Map();
+  for (const line of readFileSync(path.join(CACHE, 'baby-names.csv'), 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).slice(1)) {
+    const m = /^\d+,"([A-Za-z]+)",([\d.e-]+)/.exec(line);
+    if (m) { const k = m[1].toLowerCase(); everyGiven.add(k); maxPct.set(k, Math.max(maxPct.get(k) || 0, Number(m[2]))); }
+  }
+  // Same floor, fuller data. Measured before choosing it: at 1 in 10,000 the full file holds back 23 more English
+  // words than the 2008 list (2 of them common — "dream", "promise"); at 1 in 50,000 it is 153, and the extra ones are
+  // mostly data-entry noise ("media", "author", "less"); at 1 in 100,000, "every" and "other".
+  for (const line of readFileSync(path.join(CACHE, 'ssa-babynames-1880-2017.csv'), 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).slice(1)) {
+    const c = line.split(',');
+    if (c.length < 5 || !/^[A-Za-z]+$/.test(c[2])) continue;
+    const k = c[2].toLowerCase(), p = Number(c[4]);
+    everyGiven.add(k);
+    if (p > (maxPct.get(k) || 0)) maxPct.set(k, p);
+  }
+  for (const [k, pct] of maxPct) if (pct >= 1e-4) given.add(k);
+  const places = new Set();
+  const LSAD = /\s+(city and borough|consolidated government|metro government|unified government|urban county|city|town|township|village|borough|municipality|CDP|comunidad|zona urbana)(\s*\(balance\))?$/i;
+  for (const line of readFileSync(path.join(CACHE, 'places', '2023_Gaz_place_national.txt'), 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).slice(1)) {
+    const cols = line.split('\t');
+    if (cols.length < 4) continue;
+    const n = cols[3].replace(LSAD, '').trim().toLowerCase();
+    if (/^[a-z]+$/.test(n)) places.add(n);
+  }
+  // THE EVERYDAY WORDS (vocab.mjs FUNCTION_WORDS) are words wherever they are, whether or not SCOWL lists them: "quick"
+  // was in it and the list kept it out of the names; "max" was not, so it stayed a short name and was masked in "Add a
+  // 'max' subcommand" (2026-10-28, after the developer's "yes, exempt it" had added it to the list)
+  for (const w of FUNCTION_WORDS) english.add(w);
+  return { english, surnames, given, places, everyGiven };
+}
+
+/** The strings one swap of two neighbouring (different) letters away from `w`: "hte" -> "the", "eht". */
+export function swaps(w) {
+  const out = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < w.length; i++) if (w[i] !== w[i + 1]) out.push(w.slice(0, i) + w[i + 1] + w[i] + w.slice(i + 2));
+  return out;
+}
+
+/**
+ * TYPO NAMES: every name, of any rarity - all 2010 surnames, every given name in either source, every place - that is
+ * NOT an English word but is one swap away from one. The masker shows a lower-case word that is one swap from an
+ * English word ("hte", "ifles"), and must never show a name that way: "teh" and "thier" are surnames.
+ */
+export function typoNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  for (const n of [...surnames.keys(), ...everyGiven, ...places]) {
+    if (n.length >= 3 && !english.has(n) && swaps(n).some((s) => english.has(s))) out.add(n);
+  }
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/**
+ * TYPO NEAR A NAME: the strings one swap from an English word AND one swap from a name - a misspelled "karla"
+ * ("krala") reads as a slip of "kraal". The masker never shows them as a typo. The names here are every 2010 surname,
+ * the given names over the floor (the rarest given names - "Het", given to six babies in 2010 - would refuse "hte"),
+ * and every place. Measured: this refuses 1.3% of the swaps of the commonest English words, everyGiven 1.7%.
+ */
+export function typoNear({ english, surnames, given, places }) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  for (const n of [...surnames.keys(), ...given, ...places]) {
+    if (english.has(n)) continue;
+    for (const v of swaps(n)) if (v.length >= 3 && !english.has(v) && swaps(v).some((s) => english.has(s))) out.add(v);
+  }
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/**
+ * SPLIT NAMES: every name, of any rarity, that is NOT an English word but would become one by losing its first or last
+ * letter to the word beside it, or by taking one letter from it ("ewing" is "wing" after "car e"). The masker shows
+ * the two halves of a misplaced space ("yo ushould" is "you should") and must never show a name that way.
+ */
+export function splitNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  const L = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
+  for (const n of [...surnames.keys(), ...everyGiven, ...places]) {
+    if (english.has(n) || !/^[a-z]+$/.test(n)) continue;
+    let hit = n.length >= 2 && (english.has(n.slice(1)) || english.has(n.slice(0, -1)));
+    if (!hit) for (const c of L) if (english.has(c + n) || english.has(n + c)) { hit = true; break; }
+    if (hit) out.add(n);
+  }
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/**
+ * AN EXTRA LETTER (the developer, 2026-10-16: "we don't mask if removing 1 char from the word makes it one of our
+ * exempt words"). The masker shows a lower-case word that becomes a word, or a JARGON word, when one letter is taken
+ * out - never the first letter ("jsmith" is a login, not a slip of "smith"), and at least four letters must be left.
+ * True when `v` is such a string. `target` is the English words and JARGON together.
+ */
+export function dropOneLetter(v, target) {
+  if (v.length < 5) return false;
+  for (let i = 1; i < v.length; i++) if (target.has(v.slice(0, i) + v.slice(i + 1))) return true;
+  return false;
+}
+
+/**
+ * EXTRA-LETTER NAMES: every name, of any rarity, that is NOT an English word but is one letter more than a word or a
+ * JARGON word ("jones" is "ones", "laura" is "aura", "jason" is "json"). Measured: 18.4% of people by surname. Never
+ * shown as a slip of that word.
+ */
+export function delNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }, target) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  for (const n of [...surnames.keys(), ...everyGiven, ...places]) if (/^[a-z]+$/.test(n) && !english.has(n) && dropOneLetter(n, target)) out.add(n);
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/**
+ * EXTRA LETTER NEAR A NAME: a name with a letter typed into it that reads as a word with one too: "moorel" is "moore",
+ * not "morel". Never shown either. The surnames are the common ones only (SURNAME_MIN): every surname makes 166,633
+ * strings and refuses 7.0% of the slips of common words; at the floor it is about 32,000 and 2.2% (measured
+ * 2026-10-16).
+ */
+export function delNear({ english, surnames, given, places }, target, names = new Set()) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  const L = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
+  for (const n of new Set([...[...surnames].filter(([, c]) => c >= SURNAME_MIN).map(([k]) => k), ...given, ...places])) {
+    if (!/^[a-z]+$/.test(n) || english.has(n)) continue;
+    for (let i = 1; i <= n.length; i++) for (const c of L) {
+      const v = n.slice(0, i) + c + n.slice(i);
+      if (!english.has(v) && !names.has(v) && dropOneLetter(v, target)) out.add(v);
+    }
+  }
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/** Does putting one letter into `v` - never in front of its first - make a word of `target`? (four letters or more) */
+export function addOneLetter(v, target) {
+  if (v.length < 4) return false;
+  const L = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
+  for (let i = 1; i <= v.length; i++) for (const c of L) if (target.has(v.slice(0, i) + c + v.slice(i))) return true;
+  return false;
+}
+
+/**
+ * MISSING-LETTER NAMES (the developer, 2026-10-28: "ad a subcomand calld avrage-qty that prnts the averge qty per
+ * order" reached the agent with four of its words masked): every name, of any rarity, that is NOT an English word but
+ * is a word with one letter left out ("mary" is "marry"). Never shown as a slip of that word.
+ */
+export function insNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }, target) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  for (const n of [...surnames.keys(), ...everyGiven, ...places]) if (/^[a-z]+$/.test(n) && !english.has(n) && addOneLetter(n, target)) out.add(n);
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/**
+ * MISSING LETTER NEAR A NAME: a common name with a letter left out that reads as a word with one left out too. Never
+ * shown either. The surnames are the common ones only (SURNAME_MIN), as for delNear.
+ */
+export function insNear({ english, surnames, given, places }, target, names = new Set()) {
+  const out = new Set();
+  for (const n of new Set([...[...surnames].filter(([, c]) => c >= SURNAME_MIN).map(([k]) => k), ...given, ...places])) {
+    if (!/^[a-z]+$/.test(n) || english.has(n)) continue;
+    for (let i = 1; i < n.length; i++) {
+      const v = n.slice(0, i) + n.slice(i + 1);
+      if (!english.has(v) && !names.has(v) && addOneLetter(v, target)) out.add(v);
+    }
+  }
+  return [...out].sort();
+}
+
+/** Can `w` be assembled entirely from dictionary pieces of 3+ letters? */
+function segmentable(w, dict) {
+  if (w.length < 6 || w.length > 30) return false;
+  const ok = new Array(w.length + 1).fill(false);
+  ok[0] = true;
+  for (let i = 3; i <= w.length; i++) {
+    for (let j = 0; j <= i - 3; j++) {
+      if (ok[j] && dict.has(w.slice(j, i))) { ok[i] = true; break; }
+    }
+  }
+  return ok[w.length];
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  await sources();
+  const { english, surnames, given, places, everyGiven } = load();
+  const commonSurname = (w) => (surnames.get(w) || 0) >= SURNAME_MIN;
+  const ambiguous = [...english].filter((w) => w.length >= 3 && !FUNCTION_WORDS.has(w) && (commonSurname(w) || given.has(w))).sort();
+  const dict = new Set([...english, ...JARGON]);
+  const guard = new Set();
+  for (const n of [...surnames.keys(), ...given, ...places]) {
+    if (!english.has(n) && segmentable(n, dict)) guard.add(n);
+  }
+  // Same floor as the English words: "auth", "dev" and "dir" are somebody's surname, but not enough people's to hold
+  // back a word every engineer writes. "jackson" and "jenkins" are, and stay out of strings and comments.
+  const jargonCodeOnly = [...JARGON].filter((w) => (commonSurname(w) || given.has(w)) && !english.has(w)).sort();
+
+  // What the thresholds cost, where it is easiest to see: the most common words in the language.
+  const wl = path.join(CACHE, 'wordlist-english');
+  const top = new Set([...JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(wl, 'english-words-10.json'), 'utf8'))]);
+  const amb = new Set(ambiguous);
+  const topHeld = [...top].filter((w) => amb.has(w));
+  console.log(`english ${english.size}  surnames ${surnames.size} (>=${SURNAME_MIN}: ${[...surnames.values()].filter((c) => c >= SURNAME_MIN).length})  given ${given.size}  places ${places.size}`);
+  console.log(`ambiguous ${ambiguous.length}  guardNames ${guard.size}  jargonCodeOnly ${jargonCodeOnly.length}  everyGiven ${everyGiven.size}`);
+  console.log(`most-common-3,940 words held back as ambiguous: ${topHeld.length}`);
+  console.log('  sample: ' + topHeld.slice(0, 80).join(' '));
+  console.log('  jargon that is also a name (code only): ' + jargonCodeOnly.join(' '));
+
+  const out = {
+    built: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
+    surnameMin: SURNAME_MIN,
+    sources: ['SCOWL via wordlist-english@1.2.1 (sizes <=60)', 'Census 2010 surnames', 'Census 1990 given names', 'SSA baby names 1880-2008 (hadley/data-baby-names)', 'SSA baby names 1880-2017, all names (TidyTuesday mirror)', 'Census 2023 Gazetteer places'],
+    english: [...english].sort(),
+    ambiguous,
+    guardNames: [...guard].sort(),
+    jargonCodeOnly,
+    // Names of four letters or fewer. An unfamiliar three-letter piece of an identifier (`ddl`, `jsr`, `nfs`) is
+    // allowed in code — unless it is one of these.
+    shortNames: [...new Set([...given, ...[...surnames].filter(([, c]) => c >= SURNAME_MIN).map(([n]) => n)])].filter((n) => n.length <= 4 && !FUNCTION_WORDS.has(n)).sort(),
+    typoNames: typoNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }),
+    typoNear: typoNear({ english, surnames, given, places }),
+    splitNames: splitNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }).filter((n) => !FUNCTION_WORDS.has(n)),
+  };
+  out.delNames = delNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }, dict);
+  out.delNear = delNear({ english, surnames, given, places }, dict, new Set(out.delNames));
+  out.insNames = insNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven, places }, dict);
+  out.insNear = insNear({ english, surnames, given, places }, dict, new Set(out.insNames));
+  {
+    const ppl = [...surnames].reduce((a, [, c]) => a + c, 0), ins = new Set(out.insNames);
+    const hit = [...surnames].filter(([n]) => ins.has(n)).reduce((a, [, c]) => a + c, 0);
+    console.log(`missing-letter names: ${out.insNames.length} (${(100 * hit / ppl).toFixed(1)}% of people by surname), ${out.insNear.length} near a common name`);
+  }
+  // (a word a line: on one line, a word added to vocab.mjs changed the whole 1.8 MB line, and the chunk that carries the
+  // project to the box was 3.6 MB of it; 2026-11-03)
+  const text = '{\n' + Object.entries(out).map(([k, v]) => JSON.stringify(k) + ': ' + (Array.isArray(v) ? '[\n' + v.map((x) => JSON.stringify(x)).join(',\n') + '\n]' : JSON.stringify(v))).join(',\n') + '\n}\n';
+  writeFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'lexicon.json'), text);
+  console.log(`wrote lexicon.json (${(text.length / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)`);
+}
+if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
+
